# P5.3 — function-level randomized differential sweep (1.13c formulas)

Generated by `npx tsx tools/oracle/fn-sweep.ts` (seed 1). Oracle = original 1.13c
`D2Common.dll` / `D2Game.dll` functions called in-process by `tools/oracle/hook/fnharness.dll.c` inside a
running single-player game (level-1 Barbarian, Rogue Encampment); TS = the functions in
`tools/oracle/lib/fn-formulas.ts`. Calling conventions: `kb/engine/oracle-fn-harness-113c.md`.

## How to run

```sh
cp -a ~/.local/opt/d2oracle/wine/prefix ~/.local/opt/d2oracle/wine/prefix-fn   # once
npx tsx tools/oracle/fn-sweep.ts --seed 1            # oracle run + compare + goldens + this report
npx tsx tools/oracle/fn-sweep.ts --seed 1 --reuse    # re-compare against the last oracle output
npx vitest run tests/oracle/formula-golden.test.ts   # goldens, no Wine needed
```

Request lines: 3752296; compared values: 8017639.

## Mismatch classes

Class key = `category|function|argument|level band`. Each class lists the minimized counterexample
(fewest synergy points, lowest level) and the root-cause hypothesis with binary evidence.
Known-failing goldens reference the id in `tests/oracle/formula-golden.test.ts`.

### M-anim-001 `anim|diminish|v<=-120`

- mismatching values: 14036; affected: diminish
- minimized counterexample: args [0, -121] -> oracle `14520`, TS `-100`
- hypothesis: `0x6FD823E0` computes `base*v/(base+v)` with 32-bit `imul`/`idiv` (0x6FD82444..0x6FD8244D): v = -120 divides by zero (oracle "fault"), v < -120 gives large positive rates, |v| > ~17.9M wraps; `computeDiminishingRate113c` returns -100 for `120 + v <= 0` and uses doubles. Boundary only.

### M-anim-002 `anim|diminish|v>=0`

- mismatching values: 765; affected: diminish
- minimized counterexample: args [0, 1073741824] -> oracle `0`, TS `119`
- hypothesis: `0x6FD823E0` computes `base*v/(base+v)` with 32-bit `imul`/`idiv` (0x6FD82444..0x6FD8244D): v = -120 divides by zero (oracle "fault"), v < -120 gives large positive rates, |v| > ~17.9M wraps; `computeDiminishingRate113c` returns -100 for `120 + v <= 0` and uses doubles. Boundary only.

### M-block-001 `block|block|expansion+dex<15`

- mismatching values: 11042; affected: block
- minimized counterexample: args [0, 1, 1, 1, 1] -> oracle `-182`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: `#10212` clamps only the upper bound (`cmp edi,0x4b; jge` at 0x6FD81E7F) and returns negative block rates (dex < 15, negative toblock, clvl <= 0 -> divisor 2); `computeBlockRate113c` clamps to [0, 75]. No gameplay effect (rand(100) < negative is never true). Note CharStats BlockFactor is read at +0x49 (0x6FD81E41), not +0x35 as kb/combat/d2common-10212-block-rate.md states.

### M-block-004 `block|block|classic+dex<15`

- mismatching values: 1165; affected: block
- minimized counterexample: args [0, 0, 1, 1, -28] -> oracle `-3`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: `#10212` clamps only the upper bound (`cmp edi,0x4b; jge` at 0x6FD81E7F) and returns negative block rates (dex < 15, negative toblock, clvl <= 0 -> divisor 2); `computeBlockRate113c` clamps to [0, 75]. No gameplay effect (rand(100) < negative is never true). Note CharStats BlockFactor is read at +0x49 (0x6FD81E41), not +0x35 as kb/combat/d2common-10212-block-rate.md states.

### M-dmg-001 `dmg|dmg.total|player|cold|absorb`

- mismatching values: 575; affected: dmg.total
- minimized counterexample: args [1, 2, 0, 2, 38087, 0, 18, 0, 20, -73, -18, 24, 0, 0] -> oracle `57893`, TS `57910`
- hypothesis: Argument order of the absorb mulDiv: D2Game `0x6FCFAD2A..0x6FCFAD40` calls mulDiv `0x6FC214D0` with `edx = damage256`, `eax = absorb%`, so the shortcut only triggers for damage256 > 0x100000; TS `applyElementalAbsorb113c` calls `mulDiv113c(remaining256, absorbPct, 100)`, i.e. damage in the `eax` slot, so its `(a / 100) * pct` shortcut fires for any channel above 0x10000 (256 damage) and absorbs up to `pct - 1` 256ths too little (e.g. 135883 cold, 9% -> original 12229, TS 12222). GAMEPLAY-RELEVANT for every absorb% item against hits above 256 damage; one-line fix (swap the first two arguments).

### M-exp-001 `exp|expratio|exp>2^20`

- mismatching values: 36187; affected: expratio
- minimized counterexample: args [1048577, 1] -> oracle `1048576`, TS `1048577`
- hypothesis: `0x6FCFA9E0` guards the multiply: if `exp > 0x7FFFFFFF >> (shift + (ratio >> shift))` it returns `(exp >> shift) * ratio` (0x6FCFAA08..0x6FCFAA1F), dropping the low 10 bits; `applyExpRatio113c` uses `floor(exp * ratio / 1024)`. GAMEPLAY-RELEVANT: any kill worth more than 1,048,575 experience after the level-difference step (Hell bosses) gets up to 1023 exp less in the original.

### M-exp-002 `exp|explvl|exp>2^20`

- mismatching values: 31806; affected: explvl
- minimized counterexample: args [28, 33, 1048577] -> oracle `889700`, TS `889701`
- hypothesis: `0x6FCFAA40` scales through mulDiv `0x6FC214D0` (tail jump at 0x6FCFAA81) with `edx = exp`; for exp > 0x100000 the shortcut is `(exp / 256) * factor` or `(exp / mlvl) * clvl`. `computeMonsterExpGrant113c` uses exact `floor(exp * factor / 256)`. GAMEPLAY-RELEVANT for big kills, same range as expratio. (kb combat/d2game-experience-pipeline-113c.md lists `exp` as `[esp+4]`; it is `edx`.)

### M-exp-003 `exp|expratio|exp<=0`

- mismatching values: 4121; affected: expratio
- minimized counterexample: args [-1, 1] -> oracle `-1`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: TS returns 0 for exp <= 0; the binary returns the input unchanged (`test edi,edi; jle` at 0x6FCFA9E3).

### M-exp-004 `exp|expratio|clvl-out-of-range`

- mismatching values: 3867; affected: expratio
- minimized counterexample: args [7, -20] -> oracle `0`, TS `7`
- hypothesis: TS clamps clvl to 1..99; the binary reads Experience.txt ExpRatio via D2Common for the raw level (boundary).

### M-exp-005 `exp|explvl|exp<=0`

- mismatching values: 3676; affected: explvl
- minimized counterexample: args [2, 2, -3] -> oracle `-3`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: TS returns 0 for exp <= 0; the binary scales negative values (the pipeline guards exp <= 0 earlier, 0x6FCFC03A).

### M-exp-006 `exp|explvl|lvl<=0`

- mismatching values: 3166; affected: explvl
- minimized counterexample: args [3, -3, 1] -> oracle `0`, TS `1`
- hypothesis: TS clamps clvl/mlvl to >= 1; binary indexes the tables with the raw difference (boundary).

### M-exp-007 `exp|expratio|exp>cap`

- mismatching values: 1342; affected: expratio
- minimized counterexample: args [8417667, 64] -> oracle `8417280`, TS `8417667`
- hypothesis: Same overflow guard as `exp>2^20` (inputs above the 0x7FFFFF pipeline cap).

### M-exp-008 `exp|explvl|exp>cap`

- mismatching values: 1174; affected: explvl
- minimized counterexample: args [18, 63, 8479367] -> oracle `165610`, TS `165612`
- hypothesis: Same mulDiv shortcut as `exp>2^20` (inputs above the 0x7FFFFF pipeline cap).

### M-exp-009 `exp|expratio|normal`

- mismatching values: 491; affected: expratio
- minimized counterexample: args [7, 99] -> oracle `0`, TS `7`
- hypothesis: clvl 99: `#10989` (D2Common 0x6FDAE840, imported by D2Game thunk 0x6FC2A784) returns 0 for `level > MaxLvl` and otherwise the ExpRatio column of row `[0x6FDF0B50] + 0x20*(level+1)`; the original yields 0 exp at clvl 99 while `applyExpRatio113c` returns the input unchanged (ratio 1024). Unreachable in practice (the pipeline returns 0 at max level, 0x6FCFC053..0x6FCFC069).

### M-frames-001 `frames|anim.speed|GH|stat<0`

- mismatching values: 544; affected: anim.speed
- minimized counterexample: args [4, 0, -36, 100, 100, 6] -> oracle `32767`, TS `1`
- hypothesis: Negative rate: `#10819` multiplies `AnimData.speed * rate` and divides by 100 with the UNSIGNED magic `mul 0x51EB851F` (0x6FD831B4..0x6FD831BE, 0x6FD8325C..0x6FD83267), so a negative rate becomes huge and is clamped to 0x7FFF (0x6FD831C5); TS `computeStepSpeed256` clamps to >= 1. Walk/run use a signed divide (0x6FD83372..0x6FD83384) and `max(25, stat67 + dFRW)`. Boundary (needs FHR/FBR/FCR far below 0).

- minimized counterexample: args [7, 0, -137, 56, 100, 16] -> oracle `448`, TS `38`
- hypothesis: Negative rate: `#10819` multiplies `AnimData.speed * rate` and divides by 100 with the UNSIGNED magic `mul 0x51EB851F` (0x6FD831B4..0x6FD831BE, 0x6FD8325C..0x6FD83267), so a negative rate becomes huge and is clamped to 0x7FFF (0x6FD831C5); TS `computeStepSpeed256` clamps to >= 1. Walk/run use a signed divide (0x6FD83372..0x6FD83384) and `max(25, stat67 + dFRW)`. Boundary (needs FHR/FBR/FCR far below 0).

Walk/run use a signed divide (0x6FD83372..0x6FD83384) and `max(25, stat67 + dFRW)`. Boundary (needs FHR/FBR/FCR far below 0).

### M-hit-001 `hit|hit|melee+pct+bigAR`

- mismatching values: 2100; affected: hit
- minimized counterexample: args [3462241, 1666, 13, 107, 0, 600, 0, 0] -> oracle `5`, TS `21`
- hypothesis: 32-bit overflow: `0x6FCFDFE4 imul eax,eax,0x64` (AR*100) and the AR% mulDiv wrap for AR above ~21.4M or huge negative defense; TS uses doubles. Boundary only.

### M-primitives-001 `primitives|band5|overflow+ts-not-int32`

- mismatching values: 15645; affected: band5
- minimized counterexample: args [65537, 296, 279, 353, 402, -35795] -> oracle `1950081475`, TS `-2344885821`
- hypothesis: `0x6FD9DDB0` computes the 5-band sum with 32-bit `imul`/`lea` (wraps); `compute5BandScaling` uses JS doubles. Only for |inputs| > 2^20 (boundary).

### M-primitives-002 `primitives|dimret|overflow`

- mismatching values: 12498; affected: dimret
- minimized counterexample: args [2, 3, 612525286] -> oracle `-6416862`, TS `165381829`
- hypothesis: `#10115` (0x6FD9DC30) multiplies `x * (b - a)` with 32-bit `imul` (wraps) and then caps at `b`; `computeDiminishingReturnsRaw` uses doubles. `lvl = -6` divides by zero (oracle "fault"). Boundary only.

### M-resist-001 `resist|resist|monster|phys+res>=100`

- mismatching values: 3536; affected: resist
- minimized counterexample: args [0, 0, 101, 0, 0, 0] -> oracle `101`, TS `100`
- hypothesis: Monster branch: `computeEffectiveResistance` reports res >= 100 as `effectiveRes = 100` (+`isImmune`), the resolver `0x6FCFB3C0` returns the raw value (no upper cap, immunity is tested by the caller). Negative pierce: TS `Math.max(0, passiveEnemyPierce)`, binary subtracts the signed stat (0x6FCFB3F3..0x6FCFB3FC). The `res >= 100` classes are representation-only; negative pierce is boundary-only.

- hypothesis: Monster branch: `computeEffectiveResistance` reports res >= 100 as `effectiveRes = 100` (+`isImmune`), the resolver `0x6FCFB3C0` returns the raw value (no upper cap, immunity is tested by the caller). Negative pierce: TS `Math.max(0, passiveEnemyPierce)`, binary subtracts the signed stat (0x6FCFB3F3..0x6FCFB3FC). The `res >= 100` classes are representation-only; negative pierce is boundary-only.

### M-skilleval-001 `skilleval|eval.maxElemDmg256||lvl1-60`

- mismatching values: 11555; affected: skill 56, skill 157, skill 160, skill 164, skill 169, skill 172, skill 178, skill 185, skill 189, skill 191, skill 192, skill 193, … (34)
- minimized counterexample: skill 157 `UnHolyBolt`, lvl 1, x=0, stats {329:30, 330:50} -> oracle `0`, TS `1536`

### M-skilleval-002 `skilleval|eval.minElemDmg256||lvl1-60`

- mismatching values: 11525; affected: skill 56, skill 157, skill 160, skill 164, skill 169, skill 172, skill 178, skill 185, skill 189, skill 191, skill 192, skill 193, … (33)
- minimized counterexample: skill 157 `UnHolyBolt`, lvl 1, x=0, stats {329:30, 330:50} -> oracle `0`, TS `768`
- hypothesis: `evaluateSkill113c` falls back to the srvmissile EMin/EMax when the skill row has none (`registry.ts` `primaryMissileName` fallback, e.g. monster skill UnHolyBolt); `#10121`/`#11091` read only the skill row (0x6FDA049E..0x6FDA04C0) and return 0 — missile damage comes from MissCalc (`0x6FDBB360`). Field-semantics difference (TS merges skill and missile damage); also inherits the mulDiv shortcut of elmin mastery0.

### M-skills-001 `skills|phmin||lvl1-60`

- mismatching values: 11880; affected: skill 1, skill 117, skill 126, skill 154, skill 198, skill 200, skill 206, skill 207, skill 208, skill 221, skill 227, skill 229, … (33)
- minimized counterexample: skill 1 `Kick`, lvl 1, x=0 -> oracle `2560`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: `#10297` (0x6FDA1FF0) is not the flat skill damage only: skills whose Skills.txt flag byte (+0x05) matches the mask at `[0x6FDD90B0]+4` are delegated to `0x6FD9F930` (weapon/kick based damage, 0x6FDA2025..0x6FDA2037), and the `bStrBonus` path adds `stat21/22 * byte[+0x1A5] / 128`. `evaluateSkill113c().minPhysDmg` is the band-scaled MinDam only (Kick lvl 1: oracle 10<<8, TS 0). The TS damage pipeline adds weapon damage elsewhere, so this is a field-semantics difference; verify per skill before changing TS.

- hypothesis: Caster mastery is added through `0x6FD9F870`, which tail-calls the D2Common mulDiv `0x6FD511E0` (0x6FD9F8DA..0x6FD9F8E1, edx = damage256); for damage256 > 0x100000 the mulDiv shortcut computes `(v / 100) * pct` and drops the remainder. calc-ast `mastered()` uses `trunc(v * pct / 100)`. Reachable above 4096 damage (high synergy/mastery builds).

### M-skills-176 `skills|skform|aurastatcalc1:skill-ref|lvl<=0`

- mismatching values: 1; affected: skill 80
- minimized counterexample: skill 80 `Raise Skeletal Mage`, lvl -1, x=104, stats {329:30, 330:50} -> oracle `-10`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: TS clamps the skill level (`evaluateSkillCore113c` `effLvl = clamp(slvl, 1, 99)`, calc-ast `lvl = max(0, slvl)`, `computeSkillManaCost256` clamp 1..99); D2Common passes the raw level through (`#10147` 0x6FDA1070 / `#10786` 0x6FDA1BF0 only reject `lvl <= 0` in some codes, e.g. `ln12` 0x6FD51670, while `dm78` 0x6FDA1148, `#10090` 0x6FD9EA00 and the MissCalc paths do not), and 32-bit `imul` wraps at extreme levels. Unreachable in normal play (levels are clamped to 0..99 by `SKILLS_GetSkillLevel` 0x6FDA05D0): boundary-only.

### M-stats-005 `stats|def|override`

- mismatching values: 1126; affected: def
- minimized counterexample: args [1, -22, 0, 0, -45] -> oracle `-13`, TS `0`
- hypothesis: `UnitStatList.getDefense` applies `armor_override_percent` (182) before the armor % pool; `#10672` (0x6FD82890, kb combat/d2game-tohit-defense §2) applies `skill_armor_percent + item_armor_percent` first and the override afterwards -> off-by-one truncation differences.

### M-steal-002 `steal|steal.life|nonpositive`

- mismatching values: 8742; affected: steal.life
- minimized counterexample: args [7, -16, 6, 0, 100] -> oracle `0`, TS `-1`
- hypothesis: Negative or zero results: `0x6FCFB570` (life) / `0x6FCFAE70` (mana, `test ebx,ebx; jg` at 0x6FCFAE75) add nothing for amounts <= 0, while `computeLifeManaSteal113c` returns the signed amount (negative leech % or non-positive physical damage). Caller-dependent; boundary.
